\section*{Como usar este practico}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Como usar este practico}
Este material esta ordenado de menor a mayor dificultad. Primero intenta resolver cada ejercicio sin mirar el solucionario. Luego compara tu desarrollo con los pasos algebraicos.

Cada tema tiene cinco niveles:
\begin{enumerate}
\item Nivel 1: reconocimiento directo.
\item Nivel 2: signos, coeficientes y pequenos cambios.
\item Nivel 3: expresiones con mas estructura.
\item Nivel 4: combinaciones algebraicas mas exigentes.
\item Nivel 5: nivel imposible, pensado para dominar el metodo con seguridad.
\end{enumerate}
\newpage

\part*{Solucionario desarrollado paso a paso}\addcontentsline{toc}{part}{Solucionario desarrollado paso a paso}
\section*{Formato del solucionario}\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Formato del solucionario}
Las soluciones usan desarrollo algebraico. El texto se mantiene al minimo necesario.
\section{Soluciones: Simplificacion de fracciones algebraicas}
\nivel{1}{basico}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 1 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{9 x^{4}}{12 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{9 x^{4}}{12 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{9 x^{4}}{12 x^{2}}=\frac{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}=\frac{\cancel{3\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{3\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{3 x^{2} / 4}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 2 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{16 x^{6}}{20 x^{3}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{16 x^{6}}{20 x^{3}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{16 x^{6}}{20 x^{3}}=\frac{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 4\cdot x^{3}}{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 4\cdot x^{3}}{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}=\frac{\cancel{4\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 4\cdot x^{3}}{\cancel{4\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{4 x^{3} / 5}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 3 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{25 x^{5}}{30 x^{4}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{25 x^{5}}{30 x^{4}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{25 x^{5}}{30 x^{4}}=\frac{5\cdot x^{4}\cdot 5\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x^{4}\cdot 5\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}=\frac{\cancel{5\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 5\cdot x}{\cancel{5\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{5 x / 6}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 4 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{36 x^{3}}{18 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{36 x^{3}}{18 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{36 x^{3}}{18 x}=\frac{6\cdot x\cdot 6\cdot x^{2}}{6\cdot x\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x\cdot 6\cdot x^{2}}{6\cdot x\cdot 3}=\frac{\cancel{6\cdot x}\cdot 6\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{6\cdot x}\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{2 x^{2}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 5 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{14 x^{5}}{28 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{14 x^{5}}{28 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{14 x^{5}}{28 x^{2}}=\frac{7\cdot x^{2}\cdot 2\cdot x^{3}}{7\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{7\cdot x^{2}\cdot 2\cdot x^{3}}{7\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}=\frac{\cancel{7\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 2\cdot x^{3}}{\cancel{7\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{3} / 2}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 6 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{6 x^{4}}{10 x^{3}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{4}}{10 x^{3}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{4}}{10 x^{3}}=\frac{2\cdot x^{3}\cdot 3\cdot x}{2\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot x^{3}\cdot 3\cdot x}{2\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}=\frac{\cancel{2\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 3\cdot x}{\cancel{2\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{3 x / 5}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 7 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{12 x^{6}}{18 x^{4}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{6}}{18 x^{4}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{6}}{18 x^{4}}=\frac{3\cdot x^{4}\cdot 4\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x^{4}\cdot 4\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}=\frac{\cancel{3\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 4\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{3\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{2 x^{2} / 3}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 8 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{20 x^{4}}{12 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{20 x^{4}}{12 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{20 x^{4}}{12 x}=\frac{4\cdot x\cdot 5\cdot x^{3}}{4\cdot x\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x\cdot 5\cdot x^{3}}{4\cdot x\cdot 3}=\frac{\cancel{4\cdot x}\cdot 5\cdot x^{3}}{\cancel{4\cdot x}\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{5 x^{3} / 3}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 9 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{30 x^{3}}{20 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{30 x^{3}}{20 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{30 x^{3}}{20 x^{2}}=\frac{5\cdot x^{2}\cdot 6\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x^{2}\cdot 6\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}=\frac{\cancel{5\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 6\cdot x}{\cancel{5\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{3 x / 2}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 10 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{12 x^{5}}{30 x^{3}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{5}}{30 x^{3}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{5}}{30 x^{3}}=\frac{6\cdot x^{3}\cdot 2\cdot x^{2}}{6\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x^{3}\cdot 2\cdot x^{2}}{6\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}=\frac{\cancel{6\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 2\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{6\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{2 x^{2} / 5}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 11 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{21 x^{7}}{42 x^{4}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{21 x^{7}}{42 x^{4}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{21 x^{7}}{42 x^{4}}=\frac{7\cdot x^{4}\cdot 3\cdot x^{3}}{7\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{7\cdot x^{4}\cdot 3\cdot x^{3}}{7\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}=\frac{\cancel{7\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 3\cdot x^{3}}{\cancel{7\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{3} / 2}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 12 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{8 x^{2}}{6 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{8 x^{2}}{6 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{8 x^{2}}{6 x}=\frac{2\cdot x\cdot 4\cdot x}{2\cdot x\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot x\cdot 4\cdot x}{2\cdot x\cdot 3}=\frac{\cancel{2\cdot x}\cdot 4\cdot x}{\cancel{2\cdot x}\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{4 x / 3}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 13 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{15 x^{4}}{12 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{15 x^{4}}{12 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{15 x^{4}}{12 x^{2}}=\frac{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 5\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 5\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}=\frac{\cancel{3\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 5\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{3\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{5 x^{2} / 4}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 14 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{24 x^{6}}{20 x^{3}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]

\item \fitmath{\frac{24 x^{6}}{20 x^{3}}=\frac{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 6\cdot x^{3}}{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 6\cdot x^{3}}{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}=\frac{\cancel{4\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 6\cdot x^{3}}{\cancel{4\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{6 x^{3} / 5}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 15 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{10 x^{5}}{30 x^{4}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{10 x^{5}}{30 x^{4}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{10 x^{5}}{30 x^{4}}=\frac{5\cdot x^{4}\cdot 2\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x^{4}\cdot 2\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}=\frac{\cancel{5\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 2\cdot x}{\cancel{5\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x / 3}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 16 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{18 x^{3}}{18 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{18 x^{3}}{18 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{18 x^{3}}{18 x}=\frac{6\cdot x\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{6\cdot x\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{6\cdot x\cdot 3}=\frac{\cancel{6\cdot x}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{6\cdot x}\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{2}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 17 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{28 x^{5}}{28 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{28 x^{5}}{28 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{28 x^{5}}{28 x^{2}}=\frac{7\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4\cdot x^{3}}{7\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{7\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4\cdot x^{3}}{7\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4}=\frac{\cancel{7\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 4\cdot x^{3}}{\cancel{7\cdot x^{2}}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{3}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 18 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{10 x^{4}}{10 x^{3}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{10 x^{4}}{10 x^{3}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{10 x^{4}}{10 x^{3}}=\frac{2\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5\cdot x}{2\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5\cdot x}{2\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5}=\frac{\cancel{2\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 5\cdot x}{\cancel{2\cdot x^{3}}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 19 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{18 x^{6}}{18 x^{4}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{18 x^{6}}{18 x^{4}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{18 x^{6}}{18 x^{4}}=\frac{3\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x^{4}\cdot 6}=\frac{\cancel{3\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 6\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{3\cdot x^{4}}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{2}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 20 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{8 x^{4}}{12 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{8 x^{4}}{12 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{8 x^{4}}{12 x}=\frac{4\cdot x\cdot 2\cdot x^{3}}{4\cdot x\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x\cdot 2\cdot x^{3}}{4\cdot x\cdot 3}=\frac{\cancel{4\cdot x}\cdot 2\cdot x^{3}}{\cancel{4\cdot x}\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{2 x^{3} / 3}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\nivel{2}{intermedio inicial}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 21 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{9 x^{3} + 54 x^{2} + 72 x}{12 x^{3} - 48 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{9 x^{3} + 54 x^{2} + 72 x}{12 x^{3} - 48 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{9 x^{3} + 54 x^{2} + 72 x}{12 x^{3} - 48 x}=\frac{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}=\frac{\cancel{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\cancel{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 \left(x + 4\right)}{4 \left(x - 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 22 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{16 x^{3} + 128 x^{2} + 240 x}{20 x^{3} - 180 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{16 x^{3} + 128 x^{2} + 240 x}{20 x^{3} - 180 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{16 x^{3} + 128 x^{2} + 240 x}{20 x^{3} - 180 x}=\frac{4\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{4\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{4\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}=\frac{\cancel{4\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\cancel{4\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot 5\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{4 \left(x + 5\right)}{5 \left(x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 23 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{25 x^{3} + 250 x^{2} + 600 x}{15 x^{3} - 240 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{25 x^{3} + 250 x^{2} + 600 x}{15 x^{3} - 240 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{25 x^{3} + 250 x^{2} + 600 x}{15 x^{3} - 240 x}=\frac{5\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{5\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{5\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}=\frac{\cancel{5\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\cancel{5\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{5 \left(x + 6\right)}{3 \left(x - 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 24 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{12 x^{3} + 144 x^{2} + 420 x}{24 x^{3} + 96 x^{2} - 120 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{3} + 144 x^{2} + 420 x}{24 x^{3} + 96 x^{2} - 120 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{3} + 144 x^{2} + 420 x}{24 x^{3} + 96 x^{2} - 120 x}=\frac{6\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{6\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{6\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}=\frac{\cancel{6\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\cancel{6\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{x + 7}{2 \left(x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 25 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{6 x^{3} + 54 x^{2} + 108 x}{10 x^{3} + 40 x^{2} - 120 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{3} + 54 x^{2} + 108 x}{10 x^{3} + 40 x^{2} - 120 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{3} + 54 x^{2} + 108 x}{10 x^{3} + 40 x^{2} - 120 x}=\frac{2\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{2\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{2\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}=\frac{\cancel{2\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\cancel{2\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}\cdot 5\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 \left(x + 3\right)}{5 \left(x - 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 26 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{12 x^{3} + 60 x^{2} + 48 x}{9 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} - 27 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{3} + 60 x^{2} + 48 x}{9 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} - 27 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{3} + 60 x^{2} + 48 x}{9 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} - 27 x}=\frac{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}=\frac{\cancel{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\cancel{3\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{4 \left(x + 4\right)}{3 \left(x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 27 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{20 x^{3} + 140 x^{2} + 200 x}{16 x^{3} - 32 x^{2} - 128 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]

\item \fitmath{\boxed{1}}
\end{enumerate}

\nivel{4}{avanzado}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 61 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{4} y + 6 x^{4} + 15 x^{3} y + 30 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} y - 12 x^{2} - 72 x y - 144 x}{3 x^{3} y - 6 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} y + 12 x^{2} - 27 x y + 54 x + 54 y - 108} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{4} y + 6 x^{4} + 15 x^{3} y + 30 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} y - 12 x^{2} - 72 x y - 144 x}{3 x^{3} y - 6 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} y + 12 x^{2} - 27 x y + 54 x + 54 y - 108}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{4} y + 6 x^{4} + 15 x^{3} y + 30 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} y - 12 x^{2} - 72 x y - 144 x}{3 x^{3} y - 6 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} y + 12 x^{2} - 27 x y + 54 x + 54 y - 108}=\frac{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y - 2\right)}=\frac{\cancel{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}{\cancel{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{x \left(x + 4\right) \left(y + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(y - 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 62 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{4} y + 12 x^{4} + 24 x^{3} y + 72 x^{3} - 28 x^{2} y - 84 x^{2} - 240 x y - 720 x}{4 x^{3} y - 12 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} y + 36 x^{2} - 64 x y + 192 x + 192 y - 576} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4} y + 12 x^{4} + 24 x^{3} y + 72 x^{3} - 28 x^{2} y - 84 x^{2} - 240 x y - 720 x}{4 x^{3} y - 12 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} y + 36 x^{2} - 64 x y + 192 x + 192 y - 576}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4} y + 12 x^{4} + 24 x^{3} y + 72 x^{3} - 28 x^{2} y - 84 x^{2} - 240 x y - 720 x}{4 x^{3} y - 12 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} y + 36 x^{2} - 64 x y + 192 x + 192 y - 576}=\frac{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y - 3\right)}=\frac{\cancel{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)}{\cancel{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{x \left(x + 5\right) \left(y + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(y - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 63 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{5 x^{4} y + 5 x^{4} + 35 x^{3} y + 35 x^{3} - 70 x^{2} y - 70 x^{2} - 600 x y - 600 x}{5 x^{3} y - 20 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} y + 80 x^{2} - 125 x y + 500 x + 500 y - 2000} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x^{4} y + 5 x^{4} + 35 x^{3} y + 35 x^{3} - 70 x^{2} y - 70 x^{2} - 600 x y - 600 x}{5 x^{3} y - 20 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} y + 80 x^{2} - 125 x y + 500 x + 500 y - 2000}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x^{4} y + 5 x^{4} + 35 x^{3} y + 35 x^{3} - 70 x^{2} y - 70 x^{2} - 600 x y - 600 x}{5 x^{3} y - 20 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} y + 80 x^{2} - 125 x y + 500 x + 500 y - 2000}=\frac{5\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)}{5\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y - 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)}{5\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y - 4\right)}=\frac{\cancel{5\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)}{\cancel{5\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y - 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{x \left(x + 6\right) \left(y + 1\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(y - 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 64 - Simplificar}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{4} y + 4 x^{4} + 24 x^{3} y + 48 x^{3} + 58 x^{2} y + 116 x^{2} - 84 x y - 168 x}{2 x^{3} y - 2 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} y + 2 x^{2} - 72 x y + 72 x + 72 y - 72} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{4} y + 4 x^{4} + 24 x^{3} y + 48 x^{3} + 58 x^{2} y + 116 x^{2} - 84 x y - 168 x}{2 x^{3} y - 2 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} y + 2 x^{2} - 72 x y + 72 x + 72 y - 72}}

\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{4} y + 4 x^{4} + 18 x^{3} y + 36 x^{3} + 22 x^{2} y + 44 x^{2} - 42 x y - 84 x}{2 x^{3} y - 2 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} y + 2 x^{2} - 18 x y + 18 x + 18 y - 18}=\frac{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y - 1\right)}=\frac{\cancel{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot x\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}{\cancel{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{x \left(x + 7\right) \left(y + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(y - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{5} - 7 x^{4} - 38 x^{3} - x^{2} + 34 x + 8}{2 x^{5} + 7 x^{4} - 5 x^{3} - 25 x^{2} + 3 x + 18}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\section{Soluciones: Suma y resta de fracciones algebraicas}
\nivel{1}{basico}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 101 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2}{x} - \frac{3}{x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x=x,\qquad D_2=x=x}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=x}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2}{x} - \frac{3}{x}= \frac{2}{x} - \frac{3}{x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2}{x} - \frac{3}{x}=\frac{2 - 3}{x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 - 3}{x}=\frac{2 - 3}{x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 - 3}{x}=\frac{-1}{x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- 1 / x}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 102 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3}{x} + \frac{4}{x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x=x,\qquad D_2=x=x}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=x}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3}{x} + \frac{4}{x}= \frac{3}{x} + \frac{4}{x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3}{x} + \frac{4}{x}=\frac{3 + 4}{x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 + 4}{x}=\frac{3 + 4}{x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 + 4}{x}=\frac{7}{x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{7 / x}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 103 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4}{x} - \frac{5}{x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x=x,\qquad D_2=x=x}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=x}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4}{x} - \frac{5}{x}= \frac{4}{x} - \frac{5}{x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4}{x} - \frac{5}{x}=\frac{4 - 5}{x}}

\item \fitmath{\boxed{- 1 / x}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\nivel{2}{intermedio inicial}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 121 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x + 2} - \frac{2}{3 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 2=\left(x + 2\right),\qquad D_2=3 x + 6=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x + 2} - \frac{2}{3 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 - 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 - 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x + 6 - 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 6 - 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x + 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 x + 4}{3 \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 122 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x + 3} + \frac{3}{4 x + 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 3=\left(x + 3\right),\qquad D_2=4 x + 12=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x + 3} + \frac{3}{4 x + 12}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 + 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 + 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 12 + 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 12 + 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 15}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{4 x + 15}{4 \left(x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 123 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 1}{x + 4} - \frac{4}{5 x + 20} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 4=\left(x + 4\right),\qquad D_2=5 x + 20=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 1}{x + 4} - \frac{4}{5 x + 20}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5 - 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5 - 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 5 - 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x + 5 - 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 1}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{5 x + 1}{5 \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 124 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x + 5} + \frac{5}{2 x + 10} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 5=\left(x + 5\right),\qquad D_2=2 x + 10=\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x + 5} + \frac{5}{2 x + 10}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 + 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 + 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 4 + 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 4 + 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 9}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x + 9}{2 \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 125 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x + 1} - \frac{6}{3 x + 3} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 1=\left(x + 1\right),\qquad D_2=3 x + 3=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x + 1} - \frac{6}{3 x + 3}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3 - 6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3 - 6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x + 9 - 6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 9 - 6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x + 3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{1}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 126 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 1}{x + 2} + \frac{1}{4 x + 8} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 2=\left(x + 2\right),\qquad D_2=4 x + 8=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 1}{x + 2} + \frac{1}{4 x + 8}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{1}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{1}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 + 1}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 + 1}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 4 + 1}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 4 + 1}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 5}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{4 x + 5}{4 \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 127 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x + 3} - \frac{2}{5 x + 15} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 3=\left(x + 3\right),\qquad D_2=5 x + 15=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x + 3} - \frac{2}{5 x + 15}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{2}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{2}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5 - 2}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5 - 2}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 10 - 2}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x + 10 - 2}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 8}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{5 x + 8}{5 \left(x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 128 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x + 4} + \frac{3}{2 x + 8} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 4=\left(x + 4\right),\qquad D_2=2 x + 8=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x + 4} + \frac{3}{2 x + 8}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 + 3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 + 3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 6 + 3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 6 + 3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 9}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x + 9}{2 \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 129 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 1}{x + 5} - \frac{4}{3 x + 15} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 5=\left(x + 5\right),\qquad D_2=3 x + 15=\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 1}{x + 5} - \frac{4}{3 x + 15}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3 - 4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3 - 4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x + 3 - 4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 3 - 4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x - 1}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 x - 1}{3 \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 130 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x + 1} + \frac{5}{4 x + 4} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 1=\left(x + 1\right),\qquad D_2=4 x + 4=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x + 1} + \frac{5}{4 x + 4}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4 + 5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4 + 5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 8 + 5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 8 + 5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 13}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{4 x + 13}{4 \left(x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 131 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x + 2} - \frac{6}{5 x + 10} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 2=\left(x + 2\right),\qquad D_2=5 x + 10=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x + 2} - \frac{6}{5 x + 10}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{6}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{6}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5 - 6}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5 - 6}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 15 - 6}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x + 15 - 6}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 9}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{5 x + 9}{5 \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 132 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 1}{x + 3} + \frac{1}{2 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 3=\left(x + 3\right),\qquad D_2=2 x + 6=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 1}{x + 3} + \frac{1}{2 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{1}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{1}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2 + 1}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2 + 1}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 2 + 1}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 2 + 1}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x + 3}{2 \left(x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 133 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x + 4} - \frac{2}{3 x + 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 4=\left(x + 4\right),\qquad D_2=3 x + 12=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x + 4} - \frac{2}{3 x + 12}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 - 2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 - 2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x + 6 - 2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 6 - 2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x + 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 x + 4}{3 \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 134 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x + 5} + \frac{3}{4 x + 20} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 5=\left(x + 5\right),\qquad D_2=4 x + 20=\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x + 5} + \frac{3}{4 x + 20}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 + 3}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 + 3}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 12 + 3}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 12 + 3}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 15}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{4 x + 15}{4 \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 135 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 1}{x + 1} - \frac{4}{5 x + 5} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 1=\left(x + 1\right),\qquad D_2=5 x + 5=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 1}{x + 1} - \frac{4}{5 x + 5}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5 - 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5 - 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 5 - 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x + 5 - 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 1}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{5 x + 1}{5 \left(x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 136 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x + 2} + \frac{5}{2 x + 4} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 2=\left(x + 2\right),\qquad D_2=2 x + 4=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x + 2} + \frac{5}{2 x + 4}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{5}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{5}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 + 5}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 + 5}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 4 + 5}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 4 + 5}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 9}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x + 9}{2 \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 137 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x + 3} - \frac{6}{3 x + 9} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 3=\left(x + 3\right),\qquad D_2=3 x + 9=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x + 3} - \frac{6}{3 x + 9}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3} - \frac{6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3 - 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3 - 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x + 9 - 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 9 - 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}=\frac{3 x + 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{x + 1}{x + 3}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 138 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 1}{x + 4} + \frac{1}{4 x + 16} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 4=\left(x + 4\right),\qquad D_2=4 x + 16=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 1}{x + 4} + \frac{1}{4 x + 16}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{1}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4} + \frac{1}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 + 1}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 + 1}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 4 + 1}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 4 + 1}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}=\frac{4 x + 5}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{4 x + 5}{4 \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 139 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x + 5} - \frac{2}{5 x + 25} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 5=\left(x + 5\right),\qquad D_2=5 x + 25=\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x + 5} - \frac{2}{5 x + 25}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{2}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5} - \frac{2}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5 - 2}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5 - 2}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 10 - 2}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x + 10 - 2}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}=\frac{5 x + 8}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{5 x + 8}{5 \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 140 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x + 1} + \frac{3}{2 x + 2} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x + 1=\left(x + 1\right),\qquad D_2=2 x + 2=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x + 1} + \frac{3}{2 x + 2}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 + 3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 + 3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 6 + 3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 6 + 3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}=\frac{2 x + 9}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x + 9}{2 \left(x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\nivel{3}{intermedio}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 141 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - x - 6} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 5 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - x - 6=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 5 x + 6=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - x - 6} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9 - x^{2} - 5 x + 6}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9 - x^{2} - 5 x + 6}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{11 x + 3}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{11 x + 3}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 142 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - x - 12} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - x - 12=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 7 x + 12=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - x - 12} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 16 + x^{2} - 7 x + 12}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 16 + x^{2} - 7 x + 12}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} + x + 28}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x^{2} + x + 28}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 143 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - x - 20} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 9 x + 20} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - x - 20=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 9 x + 20=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - x - 20} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10 - x^{2} - 9 x + 20}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10 - x^{2} - 9 x + 20}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{16 x - 10}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 \left(8 x - 5\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 144 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 5 x - 6} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 7 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 5 x - 6=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 7 x + 6=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 5 x - 6} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 7 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18 + x^{2} - 7 x + 6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18 + x^{2} - 7 x + 6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} + 2 x + 24}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 \left(x^{2} + x + 12\right)}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 145 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - 4} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 9 x + 14} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 4=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 9 x + 14=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - 4} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 28 - x^{2} - 4 x + 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 28 - x^{2} - 4 x + 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{15 x + 24}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 \left(5 x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 7\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 146 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - 9} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 5 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 9=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 5 x + 6=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - 9} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 4 x + 4 + x^{2} - 6 x + 9}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 4 x + 4 + x^{2} - 6 x + 9}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} - 2 x + 13}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x^{2} - 2 x + 13}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 147 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 16} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 16=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 7 x + 12=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 16} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9 - x^{2} - 8 x + 16}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9 - x^{2} - 8 x + 16}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{14 x - 7}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{7 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 148 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - 4 x - 5} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 5 x + 4} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 4 x - 5=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 5 x + 4=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - 4 x - 5} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 5 x + 4}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 16 + x^{2} - 6 x + 5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 16 + x^{2} - 6 x + 5}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} + 2 x + 21}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x^{2} + 2 x + 21}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 149 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - 4 x - 12} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 7 x + 10} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 4 x - 12=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 7 x + 10=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - 4 x - 12} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 7 x + 10}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10 - x^{2} - 8 x + 12}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10 - x^{2} - 8 x + 12}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{15 x - 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{15 x - 2}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 150 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x^{2} + x - 6} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 9 x + 18} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} + x - 6=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 9 x + 18=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x^{2} + x - 6} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18 + x^{2} - 5 x + 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18 + x^{2} - 5 x + 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} + 4 x + 24}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 \left(x^{2} + 2 x + 12\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 6\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 151 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 4}{x^{2} + x - 12} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 11 x + 28} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} + x - 12=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 11 x + 28=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 4}{x^{2} + x - 12} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 11 x + 28}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 28 - x^{2} - 7 x + 12}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 28 - x^{2} - 7 x + 12}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{18 x + 16}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 \left(9 x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 7\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 152 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - 3 x - 4} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 3 x + 2} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 3 x - 4=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 3 x + 2=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - 3 x - 4} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 3 x + 2}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 4 x + 4 + x^{2} - 5 x + 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 4 x + 4 + x^{2} - 5 x + 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} - x + 8}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x^{2} - x + 8}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 153 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 3 x - 10} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 5 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 3 x - 10=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 5 x + 6=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 3 x - 10} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9 - x^{2} - 7 x + 10}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9 - x^{2} - 7 x + 10}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{13 x - 1}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{13 x - 1}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 154 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - 3 x - 18} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 3 x - 18=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 7 x + 12=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - 3 x - 18} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 16 + x^{2} - 9 x + 18}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 16 + x^{2} - 9 x + 18}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} - x + 34}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x^{2} - x + 34}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 155 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x^{2} + 2 x - 8} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 9 x + 20} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} + 2 x - 8=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 9 x + 20=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x^{2} + 2 x - 8} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10 - x^{2} - 6 x + 8}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10 - x^{2} - 6 x + 8}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{13 x + 2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{13 x + 2}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 156 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 7 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 2 x - 3=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 7 x + 6=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 7 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18 + x^{2} - 4 x + 3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18 + x^{2} - 4 x + 3}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} + 5 x + 21}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x^{2} + 5 x + 21}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 157 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - 2 x - 8} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 9 x + 14} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 2 x - 8=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 9 x + 14=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - 2 x - 8} - \frac{x - 2}{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) - \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 28 - x^{2} - 6 x + 8}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 28 - x^{2} - 6 x + 8}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{17 x + 20}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{17 x + 20}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 7\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 158 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - 2 x - 15} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 5 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 2 x - 15=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 5 x + 6=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 2}{x^{2} - 2 x - 15} + \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right) + \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 4 x + 4 + x^{2} - 8 x + 15}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 4 x + 4 + x^{2} - 8 x + 15}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} - 4 x + 19}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x^{2} - 4 x + 19}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 159 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 2 x - 24} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - 2 x - 24=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 7 x + 12=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 3}{x^{2} - 2 x - 24} - \frac{x - 4}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} - \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) - \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9 - x^{2} - 10 x + 24}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9 - x^{2} - 10 x + 24}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{16 x - 15}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{16 x - 15}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 160 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - x - 2} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 5 x + 4} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=x^{2} - x - 2=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right),\qquad D_2=x^{2} + 5 x + 4=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x + 4}{x^{2} - x - 2} + \frac{x - 1}{x^{2} + 5 x + 4}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) + \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 16 + x^{2} - 3 x + 2}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 16 + x^{2} - 3 x + 2}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} + 5 x + 18}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x^{2} + 5 x + 18}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\nivel{4}{avanzado}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 161 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x - 3}{3 x^{2} + 3 x - 18} - \frac{3 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 12 x - 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{2} + 3 x - 18=3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right),\qquad D_2=9 x^{2} - 12 x - 12=3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x - 3}{3 x^{2} + 3 x - 18} - \frac{3 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 12 x - 12}= \frac{\left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
