\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}=\frac{\left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right) - \left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right) - \left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}=\frac{6 x^{2} - 5 x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 11 x + 6}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{2} - 5 x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 11 x + 6}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}=\frac{3 x^{2} - 16 x - 12}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{x - 6}{3 \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 162 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x - 4}{4 x^{2} + 4 x - 48} + \frac{4 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 36 x - 36} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{2} + 4 x - 48=4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right),\qquad D_2=16 x^{2} - 36 x - 36=4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x - 4}{4 x^{2} + 4 x - 48} + \frac{4 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 36 x - 36}= \frac{\left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}=\frac{\left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right) + \left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right) + \left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}=\frac{12 x^{2} - 7 x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 19 x + 12}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{2} - 7 x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 19 x + 12}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}=\frac{16 x^{2} + 12 x}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{x}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 163 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x - 5}{2 x^{2} + 2 x - 40} - \frac{5 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 8 x - 32} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{2} + 2 x - 40=2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right),\qquad D_2=4 x^{2} - 8 x - 32=2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x - 5}{2 x^{2} + 2 x - 40} - \frac{5 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 8 x - 32}= \frac{\left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{\left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right) - \left(5 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right) - \left(5 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{8 x^{2} + 6 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 26 x + 5}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{8 x^{2} + 6 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 26 x + 5}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{3 x^{2} - 20 x - 25}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 x^{2} - 20 x - 25}{4 \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 164 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{5 x - 2}{3 x^{2} + 15 x - 18} + \frac{2 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 6 x - 3} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{2} + 15 x - 18=3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right),\qquad D_2=9 x^{2} - 6 x - 3=3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x - 2}{3 x^{2} + 15 x - 18} + \frac{2 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 6 x - 3}= \frac{\left(5 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)} + \frac{2 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(5 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)} + \frac{2 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}=\frac{\left(5 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right) + 2 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(5 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right) + 2 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}=\frac{15 x^{2} - x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 14 x + 12}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{15 x^{2} - x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 14 x + 12}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}=\frac{17 x^{2} + 13 x + 10}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{17 x^{2} + 13 x + 10}{3 \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 6\right) \left(3 x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 165 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x - 3}{4 x^{2} - 16} - \frac{3 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 24 x - 16} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{2} - 16=4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right),\qquad D_2=16 x^{2} - 24 x - 16=4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x - 3}{4 x^{2} - 16} - \frac{3 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 24 x - 16}= \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)} - \frac{3 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)} - \frac{3 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}=\frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right) - 3 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right) - 3 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}=\frac{4 x^{2} - 10 x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 9 x + 6}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{2} - 10 x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 9 x + 6}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}=\frac{x^{2} - 19 x - 12}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{x^{2} - 19 x - 12}{8 \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(2 x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 166 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x - 4}{2 x^{2} - 18} + \frac{4 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 6 x - 18} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{2} - 18=2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right),\qquad D_2=4 x^{2} - 6 x - 18=2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x - 4}{2 x^{2} - 18} + \frac{4 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 6 x - 18}= \frac{2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}=\frac{2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right) + \left(4 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right) + \left(4 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}=\frac{4 x^{2} - 2 x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 13 x + 3}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{2} - 2 x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 13 x + 3}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}=\frac{8 x^{2} + 11 x - 9}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{8 x^{2} + 11 x - 9}{2 \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(2 x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 167 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x - 5}{3 x^{2} - 48} - \frac{5 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 24 x - 48} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{2} - 48=3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right),\qquad D_2=9 x^{2} - 24 x - 48=3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x - 5}{3 x^{2} - 48} - \frac{5 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 24 x - 48}= \frac{\left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}=\frac{\left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right) - \left(5 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right) - \left(5 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}=\frac{9 x^{2} - 3 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 22 x + 8}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{9 x^{2} - 3 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 22 x + 8}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}=\frac{4 x^{2} - 25 x - 28}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{4 x^{2} - 25 x - 28}{3 \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(3 x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 168 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x - 2}{4 x^{2} + 16 x - 20} + \frac{2 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 12 x - 4} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{2} + 16 x - 20=4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right),\qquad D_2=16 x^{2} - 12 x - 4=4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x - 2}{4 x^{2} + 16 x - 20} + \frac{2 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 12 x - 4}= \frac{2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)} + \frac{\left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)} + \frac{\left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}=\frac{2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right) + \left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right) + \left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}=\frac{16 x^{2} - 4 x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 13 x + 15}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{16 x^{2} - 4 x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 13 x + 15}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}=\frac{18 x^{2} + 9 x + 13}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{18 x^{2} + 9 x + 13}{4 \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 5\right) \left(4 x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 169 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{5 x - 3}{2 x^{2} + 8 x - 24} - \frac{3 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 4 x - 8} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{2} + 8 x - 24=2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right),\qquad D_2=4 x^{2} - 4 x - 8=2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x - 3}{2 x^{2} + 8 x - 24} - \frac{3 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 4 x - 8}= \frac{\left(5 x - 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(5 x - 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{\left(5 x - 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right) - \left(3 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(5 x - 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right) - \left(3 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{10 x^{2} + 4 x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 19 x + 6}{2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{10 x^{2} + 4 x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 19 x + 6}{2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{7 x^{2} - 15 x - 12}{2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{7 x^{2} - 15 x - 12}{4 \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 170 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x - 4}{3 x^{2} - 3 x - 18} + \frac{4 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 18 x - 27} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{2} - 3 x - 18=3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right),\qquad D_2=9 x^{2} - 18 x - 27=3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x - 4}{3 x^{2} - 3 x - 18} + \frac{4 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 18 x - 27}= \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)} + \frac{2 \left(2 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)} + \frac{2 \left(2 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right) + 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right) + 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{3 x^{2} - 9 x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 10 x + 4}{3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{2} - 9 x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 10 x + 4}{3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{7 x^{2} + x - 8}{3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(7 x + 8\right)}{9 \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 171 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x - 5}{4 x^{2} - 4 x - 48} - \frac{5 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 48 x - 64} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{2} - 4 x - 48=4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right),\qquad D_2=16 x^{2} - 48 x - 64=4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x - 5}{4 x^{2} - 4 x - 48} - \frac{5 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 48 x - 64}= \frac{\left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{\left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right) - \left(5 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right) - \left(5 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{8 x^{2} - 12 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 18 x + 9}{4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{8 x^{2} - 12 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 18 x + 9}{4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{3 x^{2} - 30 x - 29}{4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 x^{2} - 30 x - 29}{16 \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 172 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x - 2}{2 x^{2} + 6 x - 8} + \frac{2 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 2 x - 2} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{2} + 6 x - 8=2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right),\qquad D_2=4 x^{2} - 2 x - 2=2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x - 2}{2 x^{2} + 6 x - 8} + \frac{2 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 2 x - 2}= \frac{\left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)} + \frac{\left(2 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)} + \frac{\left(2 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}=\frac{\left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right) + \left(2 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right) + \left(2 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}=\frac{6 x^{2} - x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 9 x + 4}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{2} - x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 9 x + 4}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}=\frac{8 x^{2} + 8 x + 2}{2\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 x + 1}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 173 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x - 3}{3 x^{2} + 9 x - 30} - \frac{3 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 12 x - 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{2} + 9 x - 30=3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right),\qquad D_2=9 x^{2} - 12 x - 12=3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x - 3}{3 x^{2} + 9 x - 30} - \frac{3 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 12 x - 12}= \frac{\left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}=\frac{\left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right) - \left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right) - \left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}=\frac{12 x^{2} - x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 17 x + 10}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{2} - x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 17 x + 10}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}=\frac{9 x^{2} - 18 x - 16}{3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 x - 8}{3 \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 174 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{5 x - 4}{4 x^{2} + 12 x - 72} + \frac{4 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 36 x - 36} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{2} + 12 x - 72=4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right),\qquad D_2=16 x^{2} - 36 x - 36=4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x - 4}{4 x^{2} + 12 x - 72} + \frac{4 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 36 x - 36}= \frac{\left(5 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(5 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}=\frac{\left(5 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right) + \left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(5 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right) + \left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}=\frac{20 x^{2} - x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 27 x + 18}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{20 x^{2} - x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 27 x + 18}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}=\frac{24 x^{2} + 26 x + 6}{4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 x + 1}{2 \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 6\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 175 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x - 5}{2 x^{2} - 4 x - 16} - \frac{5 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 8 x - 32} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{2} - 4 x - 16=2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right),\qquad D_2=4 x^{2} - 8 x - 32=2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x - 5}{2 x^{2} - 4 x - 16} - \frac{5 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 8 x - 32}= \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right) - \left(5 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right) - \left(5 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{2 x^{2} - 6 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 11 x + 2}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{2} - 6 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 11 x + 2}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{- 3 x^{2} - 17 x - 22}{2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- \frac{3 x + 11}{4 \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 176 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x - 2}{3 x^{2} + 6 x - 9} + \frac{2 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 6 x - 3} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{2} + 6 x - 9=3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right),\qquad D_2=9 x^{2} - 6 x - 3=3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x - 2}{3 x^{2} + 6 x - 9} + \frac{2 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 6 x - 3}= \frac{2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)} + \frac{2 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)} + \frac{2 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}=\frac{2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right) + 2 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right) + 2 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}=\frac{6 x^{2} - 4 x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 8 x + 6}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{2} - 4 x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 8 x + 6}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}=\frac{8 x^{2} + 4 x + 4}{3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{4 \left(2 x^{2} + x + 1\right)}{3 \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(3 x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 177 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x - 3}{4 x^{2} + 8 x - 32} - \frac{3 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 24 x - 16} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{2} + 8 x - 32=4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right),\qquad D_2=16 x^{2} - 24 x - 16=4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x - 3}{4 x^{2} + 8 x - 32} - \frac{3 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 24 x - 16}= \frac{3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)} - \frac{3 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)} - \frac{3 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}=\frac{3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right) - 3 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right) - 3 \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}=\frac{12 x^{2} - 6 x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 15 x + 12}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{12 x^{2} - 6 x - 6 - 3 x^{2} + 15 x + 12}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}=\frac{9 x^{2} - 21 x - 18}{4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 \left(x - 3\right) \left(3 x + 2\right)}{8 \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(2 x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 178 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x - 4}{2 x^{2} + 4 x - 30} + \frac{4 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 6 x - 18} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{2} + 4 x - 30=2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right),\qquad D_2=4 x^{2} - 6 x - 18=2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x - 4}{2 x^{2} + 4 x - 30} + \frac{4 x + 1}{4 x^{2} - 6 x - 18}= \frac{4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}=\frac{4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right) + \left(4 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right) + \left(4 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}=\frac{8 x^{2} + 4 x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 21 x + 5}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{8 x^{2} + 4 x - 12 + 4 x^{2} + 21 x + 5}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}=\frac{12 x^{2} + 25 x - 7}{2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(3 x + 7\right) \left(4 x - 1\right)}{2 \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 5\right) \left(2 x + 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 179 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{5 x - 5}{3 x^{2} + 6 x - 72} - \frac{5 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 24 x - 48} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{2} + 6 x - 72=3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right),\qquad D_2=9 x^{2} - 24 x - 48=3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x - 5}{3 x^{2} + 6 x - 72} - \frac{5 x + 2}{9 x^{2} - 24 x - 48}= \frac{5 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(5 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}=\frac{5 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right) - \left(5 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right) - \left(5 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}=\frac{15 x^{2} + 5 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 32 x + 12}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{15 x^{2} + 5 x - 20 - 5 x^{2} + 32 x + 12}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}=\frac{10 x^{2} - 27 x - 32}{3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{10 x^{2} - 27 x - 32}{3 \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 6\right) \left(3 x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 180 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x - 2}{4 x^{2} + 4 x - 8} + \frac{2 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 12 x - 4} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{2} + 4 x - 8=4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right),\qquad D_2=16 x^{2} - 12 x - 4=4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x - 2}{4 x^{2} + 4 x - 8} + \frac{2 x + 3}{16 x^{2} - 12 x - 4}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)} + \frac{\left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)} + \frac{\left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}=\frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right) + \left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right) + \left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}=\frac{4 x^{2} - 7 x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 7 x + 6}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{2} - 7 x - 2 + 2 x^{2} + 7 x + 6}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}=\frac{6 x^{2} + 4}{4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 x^{2} + 2}{2 \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(4 x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\nivel{5}{imposible}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 181 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{2} + 3 x - 2}{3 x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} - 28 x + 24} - \frac{3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2}{x^{4} + 7 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 28 x - 48} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} - 28 x + 24=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 7 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 28 x - 48=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{2} + 3 x - 2}{3 x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} - 28 x + 24} - \frac{3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2}{x^{4} + 7 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 28 x - 48}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{\left(x + 2\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) - \left(3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) - \left(3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{2 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} + 10 x - 8 - 9 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} + 10 x - 4}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} + 10 x - 8 - 9 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} + 10 x - 4}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{- 7 x^{3} + 23 x^{2} - 4}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- \frac{7 x^{3} - 23 x^{2} + 4}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(3 x - 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 182 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{2} + 4 x - 3}{4 x^{4} + 13 x^{3} - 48 x^{2} - 117 x + 108} + \frac{4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3}{x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} - 81 x - 180} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{4} + 13 x^{3} - 48 x^{2} - 117 x + 108=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} - 81 x - 180=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{2} + 4 x - 3}{4 x^{4} + 13 x^{3} - 48 x^{2} - 117 x + 108} + \frac{4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3}{x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} - 81 x - 180}= \frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) + \left(4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) + \left(4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{3 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} + 17 x - 15 + 16 x^{3} - 24 x^{2} + 21 x - 9}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} + 17 x - 15 + 16 x^{3} - 24 x^{2} + 21 x - 9}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{19 x^{3} - 5 x^{2} + 38 x - 24}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{19 x^{3} - 5 x^{2} + 38 x - 24}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 5\right) \left(4 x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 183 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{2} + 2 x - 4}{2 x^{4} - 40 x^{2} + 128} - \frac{2 x^{2} - 4 x + 4}{x^{4} + 8 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 128 x - 192} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{4} - 40 x^{2} + 128=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 8 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 128 x - 192=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{2} + 2 x - 4}{2 x^{4} - 40 x^{2} + 128} - \frac{2 x^{2} - 4 x + 4}{x^{4} + 8 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 128 x - 192}= \frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} + x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} - \frac{2 \left(x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} + x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} - \frac{2 \left(x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} + x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) - 2 \left(x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} + x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) - 2 \left(x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{4 x^{3} + 26 x^{2} + 8 x - 24 - 4 x^{3} - 16 x^{2} + 24 x - 16}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} + 26 x^{2} + 8 x - 24 - 4 x^{3} - 16 x^{2} + 24 x - 16}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{42 x^{2} - 16 x - 8}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(3 x - 2\right) \left(7 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 6\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 184 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 5}{3 x^{4} + 4 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} - 4 x + 15} + \frac{3 x^{2} - x + 5}{x^{4} + 10 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} - 10 x - 21} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{4} + 4 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} - 4 x + 15=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 10 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} - 10 x - 21=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 5}{3 x^{4} + 4 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} - 4 x + 15} + \frac{3 x^{2} - x + 5}{x^{4} + 10 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} - 10 x - 21}= \frac{\left(x^{2} + 3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} + \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x^{2} + 3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} + \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{\left(x^{2} + 3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) + \left(3 x^{2} - x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x^{2} + 3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) + \left(3 x^{2} - x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{x^{3} + 10 x^{2} + 16 x - 35 + 9 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} + 20 x - 25}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{3} + 10 x^{2} + 16 x - 35 + 9 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} + 20 x - 25}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{10 x^{3} - 8 x^{2} + 36 x - 60}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 \left(5 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} + 18 x - 30\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 7\right) \left(3 x - 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 185 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{2} + 4 x - 1}{4 x^{4} + 15 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} - 60 x + 16} - \frac{4 x^{2} - 2 x + 6}{x^{4} + 12 x^{3} + 28 x^{2} - 48 x - 128} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{4} + 15 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} - 60 x + 16=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 12 x^{3} + 28 x^{2} - 48 x - 128=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{2} + 4 x - 1}{4 x^{4} + 15 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} - 60 x + 16} - \frac{4 x^{2} - 2 x + 6}{x^{4} + 12 x^{3} + 28 x^{2} - 48 x - 128}= \frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)} - \frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)} - \frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}=\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right) - 2 \left(2 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right) - 2 \left(2 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}=\frac{2 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} + 31 x - 8 - 16 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} + 26 x - 6}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} + 31 x - 8 - 16 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} + 26 x - 6}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}=\frac{- 14 x^{3} + 32 x^{2} + 5 x - 2}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- \frac{14 x^{3} - 32 x^{2} - 5 x + 2}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 8\right) \left(4 x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 186 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{2} + 2 x - 2}{2 x^{4} + 2 x^{3} - 22 x^{2} - 18 x + 36} + \frac{2 x^{2} - 3 x + 1}{x^{4} + 5 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 45 x - 54} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{4} + 2 x^{3} - 22 x^{2} - 18 x + 36=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 5 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 45 x - 54=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{2} + 2 x - 2}{2 x^{4} + 2 x^{3} - 22 x^{2} - 18 x + 36} + \frac{2 x^{2} - 3 x + 1}{x^{4} + 5 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 45 x - 54}= \frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 2 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 2 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 2 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) + \left(x - 1\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 2 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) + \left(x - 1\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{3 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} + 4 x - 6 + 4 x^{3} - 10 x^{2} + 8 x - 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} + 4 x - 6 + 4 x^{3} - 10 x^{2} + 8 x - 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{7 x^{3} + x^{2} + 12 x - 8}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{7 x^{3} + x^{2} + 12 x - 8}{2 \left(x - 3\right) \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)^{2}}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 187 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{2} + 3 x - 3}{3 x^{4} + 6 x^{3} - 57 x^{2} - 96 x + 144} - \frac{3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2}{x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 112 x - 192} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{4} + 6 x^{3} - 57 x^{2} - 96 x + 144=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 112 x - 192=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{2} + 3 x - 3}{3 x^{4} + 6 x^{3} - 57 x^{2} - 96 x + 144} - \frac{3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2}{x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 112 x - 192}= \frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 3 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 3 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 3 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) - \left(3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 3 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) - \left(3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{4 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} + 9 x - 12 - 9 x^{3} - 21 x^{2} + 18 x - 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} + 9 x - 12 - 9 x^{3} - 21 x^{2} + 18 x - 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{- 5 x^{3} + 40 x^{2} - 9 x - 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- \frac{5 x^{3} - 40 x^{2} + 9 x + 6}{3 \left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)^{2}}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 188 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 4}{4 x^{4} + 12 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} - 12 x + 16} + \frac{4 x^{2} - x + 3}{x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} - 9 x - 20} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{4} + 12 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} - 12 x + 16=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} - 9 x - 20=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 4}{4 x^{4} + 12 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} - 12 x + 16} + \frac{4 x^{2} - x + 3}{x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} - 9 x - 20}= \frac{\left(x^{2} + 4 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x^{2} + 4 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{\left(x^{2} + 4 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) + \left(4 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x^{2} + 4 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) + \left(4 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{x^{3} + 9 x^{2} + 16 x - 20 + 16 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} + 16 x - 12}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{3} + 9 x^{2} + 16 x - 20 + 16 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} + 16 x - 12}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{17 x^{3} - 11 x^{2} + 32 x - 32}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{17 x^{3} - 11 x^{2} + 32 x - 32}{4 \left(x - 1\right)^{2} \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 189 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{2} + 2 x - 5}{2 x^{4} - x^{3} - 18 x^{2} + 4 x + 40} - \frac{2 x^{2} - 2 x + 4}{x^{4} + 8 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 32 x - 48} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{4} - x^{3} - 18 x^{2} + 4 x + 40=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 8 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 32 x - 48=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{2} + 2 x - 5}{2 x^{4} - x^{3} - 18 x^{2} + 4 x + 40} - \frac{2 x^{2} - 2 x + 4}{x^{4} + 8 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 32 x - 48}= \frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} - \frac{2 \left(x^{2} - x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} - \frac{2 \left(x^{2} - x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) - 2 \left(x^{2} - x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) - 2 \left(x^{2} - x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{2 x^{3} + 14 x^{2} + 7 x - 30 - 4 x^{3} - 14 x^{2} + 18 x - 20}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} + 14 x^{2} + 7 x - 30 - 4 x^{3} - 14 x^{2} + 18 x - 20}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{- 2 x^{3} + 28 x^{2} - 11 x - 10}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- \frac{2 x^{3} - 28 x^{2} + 11 x + 10}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)^{2} \left(x + 6\right) \left(2 x - 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 190 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{2} + 3 x - 1}{3 x^{4} + 8 x^{3} - 30 x^{2} - 72 x + 27} + \frac{3 x^{2} - 3 x + 5}{x^{4} + 10 x^{3} + 12 x^{2} - 90 x - 189} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{4} + 8 x^{3} - 30 x^{2} - 72 x + 27=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 10 x^{3} + 12 x^{2} - 90 x - 189=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{2} + 3 x - 1}{3 x^{4} + 8 x^{3} - 30 x^{2} - 72 x + 27} + \frac{3 x^{2} - 3 x + 5}{x^{4} + 10 x^{3} + 12 x^{2} - 90 x - 189}= \frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} + \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 3 x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} + \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 3 x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) + \left(3 x^{2} - 3 x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) + \left(3 x^{2} - 3 x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{3 x^{3} + 24 x^{2} + 20 x - 7 + 9 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} + 18 x - 5}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} + 24 x^{2} + 20 x - 7 + 9 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} + 18 x - 5}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{12 x^{3} + 12 x^{2} + 38 x - 12}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 \left(6 x^{3} + 6 x^{2} + 19 x - 6\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)^{2} \left(x + 7\right) \left(3 x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 191 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{2} + 4 x - 2}{4 x^{4} + 14 x^{3} - 72 x^{2} - 224 x + 128} - \frac{4 x^{2} - 4 x + 6}{x^{4} + 12 x^{3} + 16 x^{2} - 192 x - 512} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{4} + 14 x^{3} - 72 x^{2} - 224 x + 128=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 12 x^{3} + 16 x^{2} - 192 x - 512=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{2} + 4 x - 2}{4 x^{4} + 14 x^{3} - 72 x^{2} - 224 x + 128} - \frac{4 x^{2} - 4 x + 6}{x^{4} + 12 x^{3} + 16 x^{2} - 192 x - 512}= \frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} + 2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)} - \frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} - 2 x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} + 2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)} - \frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} - 2 x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}=\frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} + 2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right) - 2 \left(2 x^{2} - 2 x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} + 2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right) - 2 \left(2 x^{2} - 2 x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}=\frac{4 x^{3} + 36 x^{2} + 30 x - 16 - 16 x^{3} - 24 x^{2} + 32 x - 12}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} + 36 x^{2} + 30 x - 16 - 16 x^{3} - 24 x^{2} + 32 x - 12}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}=\frac{- 12 x^{3} + 60 x^{2} - 2 x - 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- \frac{6 x^{3} - 30 x^{2} + x + 2}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)^{2} \left(x + 8\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 192 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 2 x - 3}{2 x^{4} + x^{3} - 8 x^{2} - x + 6} + \frac{2 x^{2} - x + 1}{x^{4} + 5 x^{3} + 5 x^{2} - 5 x - 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{4} + x^{3} - 8 x^{2} - x + 6=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 5 x^{3} + 5 x^{2} - 5 x - 6=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 2 x - 3}{2 x^{4} + x^{3} - 8 x^{2} - x + 6} + \frac{2 x^{2} - x + 1}{x^{4} + 5 x^{3} + 5 x^{2} - 5 x - 6}= \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(2 x^{2} - x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(2 x^{2} - x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) + \left(2 x^{2} - x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) + \left(2 x^{2} - x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{x^{3} + 5 x^{2} + 3 x - 9 + 4 x^{3} - 8 x^{2} + 5 x - 3}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{3} + 5 x^{2} + 3 x - 9 + 4 x^{3} - 8 x^{2} + 5 x - 3}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{5 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} + 8 x - 12}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{5 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} + 8 x - 12}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(2 x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 193 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{2} + 3 x - 4}{3 x^{4} + 5 x^{3} - 24 x^{2} - 20 x + 48} - \frac{3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2}{x^{4} + 7 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 28 x - 48} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{4} + 5 x^{3} - 24 x^{2} - 20 x + 48=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 7 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 28 x - 48=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{2} + 3 x - 4}{3 x^{4} + 5 x^{3} - 24 x^{2} - 20 x + 48} - \frac{3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2}{x^{4} + 7 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 28 x - 48}= \frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) - \left(3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) - \left(3 x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{2 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} + 8 x - 16 - 9 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} + 14 x - 8}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} + 8 x - 16 - 9 x^{3} - 18 x^{2} + 14 x - 8}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{- 7 x^{3} + 29 x^{2} - 6 x - 8}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- \frac{7 x^{3} - 29 x^{2} + 6 x + 8}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(3 x - 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 194 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{2} + 4 x - 5}{4 x^{4} + 11 x^{3} - 56 x^{2} - 99 x + 180} + \frac{4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3}{x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} - 81 x - 180} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{4} + 11 x^{3} - 56 x^{2} - 99 x + 180=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} - 81 x - 180=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{2} + 4 x - 5}{4 x^{4} + 11 x^{3} - 56 x^{2} - 99 x + 180} + \frac{4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3}{x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} - 81 x - 180}= \frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) + \left(4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) + \left(4 x^{2} - 3 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{3 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} + 15 x - 25 + 16 x^{3} - 32 x^{2} + 27 x - 15}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} + 15 x - 25 + 16 x^{3} - 32 x^{2} + 27 x - 15}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{19 x^{3} - 13 x^{2} + 42 x - 40}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{19 x^{3} - 13 x^{2} + 42 x - 40}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 5\right) \left(4 x - 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 195 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{2} + 2 x - 1}{2 x^{4} + 3 x^{3} - 34 x^{2} - 48 x + 32} - \frac{2 x^{2} - 4 x + 4}{x^{4} + 8 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 128 x - 192} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{4} + 3 x^{3} - 34 x^{2} - 48 x + 32=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 8 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 128 x - 192=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{2} + 2 x - 1}{2 x^{4} + 3 x^{3} - 34 x^{2} - 48 x + 32} - \frac{2 x^{2} - 4 x + 4}{x^{4} + 8 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 128 x - 192}= \frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} - \frac{2 \left(x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)} - \frac{2 \left(x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) - 2 \left(x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right) - 2 \left(x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{4 x^{3} + 26 x^{2} + 11 x - 6 - 4 x^{3} - 10 x^{2} + 12 x - 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} + 26 x^{2} + 11 x - 6 - 4 x^{3} - 10 x^{2} + 12 x - 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{36 x^{2} - x - 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
