\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(4 x - 1\right) \left(9 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 6\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 196 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 2}{3 x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 9 x^{2} - 7 x + 6} + \frac{3 x^{2} - x + 5}{x^{4} + 10 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} - 10 x - 21} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 9 x^{2} - 7 x + 6=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 10 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} - 10 x - 21=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 2}{3 x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 9 x^{2} - 7 x + 6} + \frac{3 x^{2} - x + 5}{x^{4} + 10 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} - 10 x - 21}= \frac{\left(x^{2} + 3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} + \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x^{2} + 3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)} + \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{\left(x^{2} + 3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) + \left(3 x^{2} - x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x^{2} + 3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right) + \left(3 x^{2} - x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{x^{3} + 10 x^{2} + 19 x - 14 + 9 x^{3} - 9 x^{2} + 17 x - 10}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{3} + 10 x^{2} + 19 x - 14 + 9 x^{3} - 9 x^{2} + 17 x - 10}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}=\frac{10 x^{3} + x^{2} + 36 x - 24}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{10 x^{3} + x^{2} + 36 x - 24}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 7\right) \left(3 x - 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 197 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{2} + 4 x - 3}{4 x^{4} + 13 x^{3} - 28 x^{2} - 52 x + 48} - \frac{4 x^{2} - 2 x + 6}{x^{4} + 12 x^{3} + 28 x^{2} - 48 x - 128} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{4} + 13 x^{3} - 28 x^{2} - 52 x + 48=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 12 x^{3} + 28 x^{2} - 48 x - 128=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{2} + 4 x - 3}{4 x^{4} + 13 x^{3} - 28 x^{2} - 52 x + 48} - \frac{4 x^{2} - 2 x + 6}{x^{4} + 12 x^{3} + 28 x^{2} - 48 x - 128}= \frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)} - \frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)} - \frac{2 \left(2 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}=\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right) - 2 \left(2 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(2 x^{2} + 4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right) - 2 \left(2 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}=\frac{2 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} + 29 x - 24 - 16 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} + 30 x - 18}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} + 20 x^{2} + 29 x - 24 - 16 x^{3} - 20 x^{2} + 30 x - 18}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}=\frac{- 14 x^{3} + 40 x^{2} - x - 6}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 8\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- \frac{14 x^{3} - 40 x^{2} + x + 6}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 8\right) \left(4 x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 198 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{2} + 2 x - 4}{2 x^{4} - 26 x^{2} + 72} + \frac{2 x^{2} - 3 x + 1}{x^{4} + 5 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 45 x - 54} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=2 x^{4} - 26 x^{2} + 72=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 5 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 45 x - 54=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{2} + 2 x - 4}{2 x^{4} - 26 x^{2} + 72} + \frac{2 x^{2} - 3 x + 1}{x^{4} + 5 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 45 x - 54}= \frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 2 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 2 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)} + \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 2 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) + \left(x - 1\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(3 x^{2} + 2 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right) + \left(x - 1\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{3 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} + 2 x - 12 + 4 x^{3} - 14 x^{2} + 14 x - 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} + 11 x^{2} + 2 x - 12 + 4 x^{3} - 14 x^{2} + 14 x - 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}=\frac{7 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} + 16 x - 16}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{7 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} + 16 x - 16}{2 \left(x - 3\right) \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)^{2}}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 199 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{2} + 3 x - 5}{3 x^{4} + 4 x^{3} - 63 x^{2} - 64 x + 240} - \frac{3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2}{x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 112 x - 192} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=3 x^{4} + 4 x^{3} - 63 x^{2} - 64 x + 240=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 112 x - 192=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{2} + 3 x - 5}{3 x^{4} + 4 x^{3} - 63 x^{2} - 64 x + 240} - \frac{3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2}{x^{4} + 7 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 112 x - 192}= \frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)} - \frac{\left(3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) - \left(3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(4 x^{2} + 3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right) - \left(3 x^{2} - 4 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{4 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} + 7 x - 20 - 9 x^{3} - 27 x^{2} + 26 x - 10}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} + 7 x - 20 - 9 x^{3} - 27 x^{2} + 26 x - 10}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}=\frac{- 5 x^{3} + 46 x^{2} - 19 x - 10}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{- \frac{5 x^{3} - 46 x^{2} + 19 x + 10}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)^{2} \left(3 x - 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 200 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 1}{4 x^{4} + 15 x^{3} - 8 x^{2} - 15 x + 4} + \frac{4 x^{2} - x + 3}{x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} - 9 x - 20} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{D_1=4 x^{4} + 15 x^{3} - 8 x^{2} - 15 x + 4=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right),\qquad D_2=x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} - 9 x - 20=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\operatorname{mcm}(D_1,D_2)=\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 1}{4 x^{4} + 15 x^{3} - 8 x^{2} - 15 x + 4} + \frac{4 x^{2} - x + 3}{x^{4} + 9 x^{3} + 19 x^{2} - 9 x - 20}= \frac{\left(x^{2} + 4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x^{2} + 4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)} + \frac{\left(4 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{\left(x^{2} + 4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) + \left(4 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x^{2} + 4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right) + \left(4 x^{2} - x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{x^{3} + 9 x^{2} + 19 x - 5 + 16 x^{3} - 8 x^{2} + 13 x - 3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{3} + 9 x^{2} + 19 x - 5 + 16 x^{3} - 8 x^{2} + 13 x - 3}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{17 x^{3} + x^{2} + 32 x - 8}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{17 x^{3} + x^{2} + 32 x - 8}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 5\right) \left(4 x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\section{Soluciones: Multiplicacion y division de fracciones algebraicas}
\nivel{1}{basico}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 201 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{3}}{3 x} \div \frac{3 x}{3 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3}}{3 x} \div \frac{3 x}{3 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3}}{3 x} \div \frac{3 x}{3 x^{2}}= \frac{3\cdot x^{3}}{3\cdot x} \div \frac{3\cdot x}{3\cdot x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x^{3}}{3\cdot x}\div \frac{3\cdot x}{3\cdot x^{2}}= \frac{3\cdot x^{3}}{3\cdot x}\cdot \frac{3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x^{3}}{3\cdot x}\cdot \frac{3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x}= \frac{3\cdot x^{3}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x\cdot 3\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x^{3}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x\cdot 3\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{3}\cdot x^{3}\cdot \cancel{3}\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{3}\cdot x\cdot \cancel{3}\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{3}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 202 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{4}}{4 x} \cdot \frac{4 x}{5 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4}}{4 x} \cdot \frac{4 x}{5 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4}}{4 x} \cdot \frac{4 x}{5 x}= \frac{4\cdot x^{4}}{4\cdot x} \cdot \frac{4\cdot x}{5\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{4}}{4\cdot x}\cdot \frac{4\cdot x}{5\cdot x}= \frac{4\cdot x^{4}\cdot 4\cdot x}{4\cdot x\cdot 5\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{4}\cdot 4\cdot x}{4\cdot x\cdot 5\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{4}\cdot x^{4}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{x}}{\cancel{4}\cdot \cancel{x}\cdot 5\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{4 x^{3} / 5}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 203 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{5 x^{2}}{5 x} \div \frac{5 x}{5 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x^{2}}{5 x} \div \frac{5 x}{5 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x^{2}}{5 x} \div \frac{5 x}{5 x^{2}}= \frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x} \div \frac{5\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x}\div \frac{5\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{2}}= \frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x}\cdot \frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x}\cdot \frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x}= \frac{5\cdot x^{2}\cdot 5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x\cdot 5\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x^{2}\cdot 5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x\cdot 5\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{5}\cdot x^{2}\cdot \cancel{5}\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{5}\cdot x\cdot \cancel{5}\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{2}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 204 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{6 x^{3}}{2 x} \cdot \frac{2 x}{7 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]

\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{3}}{2 x} \cdot \frac{2 x}{7 x}= \frac{6\cdot x^{3}}{2\cdot x} \cdot \frac{2\cdot x}{7\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x^{3}}{2\cdot x}\cdot \frac{2\cdot x}{7\cdot x}= \frac{6\cdot x^{3}\cdot 2\cdot x}{2\cdot x\cdot 7\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x^{3}\cdot 2\cdot x}{2\cdot x\cdot 7\cdot x}= \frac{6\cdot x^{3}\cdot \cancel{2}\cdot \cancel{x}}{\cancel{2}\cdot \cancel{x}\cdot 7\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{6 x^{2} / 7}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 205 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{4}}{3 x} \div \frac{2 x}{3 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{4}}{3 x} \div \frac{2 x}{3 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{4}}{3 x} \div \frac{2 x}{3 x^{2}}= \frac{2\cdot x^{4}}{3\cdot x} \div \frac{2\cdot x}{3\cdot x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot x^{4}}{3\cdot x}\div \frac{2\cdot x}{3\cdot x^{2}}= \frac{2\cdot x^{4}}{3\cdot x}\cdot \frac{3\cdot x^{2}}{2\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot x^{4}}{3\cdot x}\cdot \frac{3\cdot x^{2}}{2\cdot x}= \frac{2\cdot x^{4}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x\cdot 2\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot x^{4}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x\cdot 2\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{2}\cdot x^{4}\cdot \cancel{3}\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{3}\cdot x\cdot \cancel{2}\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{4}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 206 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{2}}{4 x} \cdot \frac{4 x}{3 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]

\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{2}}{4 x} \cdot \frac{4 x}{3 x}= \frac{3\cdot x^{2}}{4\cdot x} \cdot \frac{4\cdot x}{3\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x^{2}}{4\cdot x}\cdot \frac{4\cdot x}{3\cdot x}= \frac{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4\cdot x}{4\cdot x\cdot 3\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4\cdot x}{4\cdot x\cdot 3\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{3}\cdot x^{2}\cdot \cancel{4}\cdot \cancel{x}}{\cancel{4}\cdot \cancel{x}\cdot \cancel{3}\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 207 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{3}}{5 x} \div \frac{4 x}{5 x^{2}} $\par

\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{3}}{5\cdot x}\div \frac{4\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{2}}= \frac{4\cdot x^{3}}{5\cdot x}\cdot \frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{4\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{3}}{5\cdot x}\cdot \frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{4\cdot x}= \frac{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x\cdot 4\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x\cdot 4\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{4}\cdot x^{3}\cdot \cancel{5}\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{5}\cdot x\cdot \cancel{4}\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{3}}}
\end{enumerate}

\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x^{4}\cdot 2\cdot x}{2\cdot x\cdot 5\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{5}\cdot x^{4}\cdot \cancel{2}\cdot \cancel{x}}{\cancel{2}\cdot \cancel{x}\cdot \cancel{5}\cdot x}}

\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x}\cdot \frac{3\cdot x^{2}}{6\cdot x}= \frac{6\cdot x^{2}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x\cdot 6\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x^{2}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x\cdot 6\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{6}\cdot x^{2}\cdot \cancel{3}\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{3}\cdot x\cdot \cancel{6}\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{2}}}
\end{enumerate}

\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 217 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{4}}{3 x} \div \frac{4 x}{3 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4}}{3 x} \div \frac{4 x}{3 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4}}{3 x} \div \frac{4 x}{3 x^{2}}= \frac{4\cdot x^{4}}{3\cdot x} \div \frac{4\cdot x}{3\cdot x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{4}}{3\cdot x}\div \frac{4\cdot x}{3\cdot x^{2}}= \frac{4\cdot x^{4}}{3\cdot x}\cdot \frac{3\cdot x^{2}}{4\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{4}}{3\cdot x}\cdot \frac{3\cdot x^{2}}{4\cdot x}= \frac{4\cdot x^{4}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x\cdot 4\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4\cdot x^{4}\cdot 3\cdot x^{2}}{3\cdot x\cdot 4\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{4}\cdot x^{4}\cdot \cancel{3}\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{3}\cdot x\cdot \cancel{4}\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{4}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 218 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{5 x^{2}}{4 x} \cdot \frac{4 x}{3 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x^{2}}{4 x} \cdot \frac{4 x}{3 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5 x^{2}}{4 x} \cdot \frac{4 x}{3 x}= \frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{4\cdot x} \cdot \frac{4\cdot x}{3\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{4\cdot x}\cdot \frac{4\cdot x}{3\cdot x}= \frac{5\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4\cdot x}{4\cdot x\cdot 3\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{5\cdot x^{2}\cdot 4\cdot x}{4\cdot x\cdot 3\cdot x}= \frac{5\cdot x^{2}\cdot \cancel{4}\cdot \cancel{x}}{\cancel{4}\cdot \cancel{x}\cdot 3\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{5 x / 3}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 219 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{6 x^{3}}{5 x} \div \frac{6 x}{5 x^{2}} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{3}}{5 x} \div \frac{6 x}{5 x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6 x^{3}}{5 x} \div \frac{6 x}{5 x^{2}}= \frac{6\cdot x^{3}}{5\cdot x} \div \frac{6\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{2}}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x^{3}}{5\cdot x}\div \frac{6\cdot x}{5\cdot x^{2}}= \frac{6\cdot x^{3}}{5\cdot x}\cdot \frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{6\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x^{3}}{5\cdot x}\cdot \frac{5\cdot x^{2}}{6\cdot x}= \frac{6\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x\cdot 6\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{6\cdot x^{3}\cdot 5\cdot x^{2}}{5\cdot x\cdot 6\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{6}\cdot x^{3}\cdot \cancel{5}\cdot x^{2}}{\cancel{5}\cdot x\cdot \cancel{6}\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x^{3}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 220 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{4}}{2 x} \cdot \frac{2 x}{5 x} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{4}}{2 x} \cdot \frac{2 x}{5 x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{4}}{2 x} \cdot \frac{2 x}{5 x}= \frac{2\cdot x^{4}}{2\cdot x} \cdot \frac{2\cdot x}{5\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot x^{4}}{2\cdot x}\cdot \frac{2\cdot x}{5\cdot x}= \frac{2\cdot x^{4}\cdot 2\cdot x}{2\cdot x\cdot 5\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2\cdot x^{4}\cdot 2\cdot x}{2\cdot x\cdot 5\cdot x}= \frac{\cancel{2}\cdot x^{4}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{x}}{\cancel{2}\cdot \cancel{x}\cdot 5\cdot x}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{2 x^{3} / 5}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\nivel{2}{intermedio inicial}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 221 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x + 6}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{4 x - 8} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 6}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{4 x - 8}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 6}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{4 x - 8}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 2\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 2\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 4}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{12}{x + 3}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 222 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x + 12}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + x - 12}{5 x + 15} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 12}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + x - 12}{5 x + 15}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 12}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + x - 12}{5 x + 15}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 3\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{4 \left(x + 4\right) / 5}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 223 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x + 8}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{6 x - 24} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 8}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{6 x - 24}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 8}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{6 x - 24}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 4\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 4\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot 6}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{12}{x + 5}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 224 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x + 15}{x - 1} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 5 x - 6}{3 x + 15} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 15}{x - 1} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 5 x - 6}{3 x + 15}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 15}{x - 1} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 5 x - 6}{3 x + 15}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 1\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \cancel{3}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \cancel{3}}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x + 6}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 225 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x + 4}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 7}{4 x - 8} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 4}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 7}{4 x - 8}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 4}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 7}{4 x - 8}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 4}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{16}{x + 7}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 226 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x + 4}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} - x - 6}{5 x + 10} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]

\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 4}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} - x - 6}{5 x + 10}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 3\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{2 \left(x + 2\right) / 5}}

\vspace{0.12cm}

\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 9}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9}{6 x - 24}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 4\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 4\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot 6}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}

\end{enumerate}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 2\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 4}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 230 - Resolver}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 5}}

\vspace{0.12cm}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot 6}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 233 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x + 12}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{4 x - 8} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 12}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{4 x - 8}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 12}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{4 x - 8}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 2\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 2\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 4}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{12}{x + 3}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 234 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x + 20}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + x - 12}{5 x + 25} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 20}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + x - 12}{5 x + 25}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 20}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + x - 12}{5 x + 25}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 3\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{4 \left(x + 4\right) / 5}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 235 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x + 2}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 5}{6 x - 24} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 2}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 5}{6 x - 24}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 2}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 5}{6 x - 24}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 4\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 4\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot 6}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{12}{x + 5}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 236 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x + 6}{x - 1} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 5 x - 6}{3 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 6}{x - 1} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 5 x - 6}{3 x + 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 6}{x - 1} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 5 x - 6}{3 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 1\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{3}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{3}}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{x + 6}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 237 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x + 12}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 10 x + 21}{4 x - 8} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 12}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 10 x + 21}{4 x - 8}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 12}{x - 2} \div \frac{x^{2} + 10 x + 21}{4 x - 8}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 4}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{16}{x + 7}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 238 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x + 8}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} - x - 6}{5 x + 20} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 8}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} - x - 6}{5 x + 20}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x + 8}{x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} - x - 6}{5 x + 20}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 3\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{2 \left(x + 2\right) / 5}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 239 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x + 15}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}{6 x - 24} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 15}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}{6 x - 24}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x + 15}{x - 4} \div \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}{6 x - 24}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 4\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 4\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot 6}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{18}{x + 3}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 240 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x + 4}{x - 1} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 4}{3 x + 3} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 4}{x - 1} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 4}{3 x + 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x + 4}{x - 1} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 4}{3 x + 3}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 1\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{4 \left(x + 4\right) / 3}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\nivel{3}{intermedio}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 241 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{3 x - 9} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 8 x + 4}{x^{2} - 5 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{3 x - 9} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 8 x + 4}{x^{2} - 5 x + 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{3 x - 9} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 8 x + 4}{x^{2} - 5 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 3} \div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 3}\div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{3 \left(3 x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 242 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{4 x - 16} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 13 x - 12}{x^{2} - 9} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{4 x - 16} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 13 x - 12}{x^{2} - 9}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{4 x - 16} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 13 x - 12}{x^{2} - 9}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 4} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 4}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 4\right) \left(4 x + 3\right)}{4 \left(x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 243 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{5 x - 25} \div \frac{2 x^{2} + 12 x + 16}{x^{2} - 6 x + 5} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{5 x - 25} \div \frac{2 x^{2} + 12 x + 16}{x^{2} - 6 x + 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{5 x - 25} \div \frac{2 x^{2} + 12 x + 16}{x^{2} - 6 x + 5}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 5} \div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 5}\div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot 5\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{10 \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 244 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 6}{2 x - 12} \cdot \frac{3 x^{2} - 17 x - 6}{x^{2} - x - 2} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 6}{2 x - 12} \cdot \frac{3 x^{2} - 17 x - 6}{x^{2} - x - 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 6}{2 x - 12} \cdot \frac{3 x^{2} - 17 x - 6}{x^{2} - x - 2}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 2} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 2}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 6\right)}\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 6\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 6\right) \left(3 x + 1\right)}{2 \left(x - 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 245 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}{3 x - 6} \div \frac{4 x^{2} + 10 x + 4}{x^{2} - 5 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}{3 x - 6} \div \frac{4 x^{2} + 10 x + 4}{x^{2} - 5 x + 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}{3 x - 6} \div \frac{4 x^{2} + 10 x + 4}{x^{2} - 5 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3} \div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3}\div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 7\right)}{6 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 246 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{4 x - 12} \cdot \frac{2 x^{2} - 3 x - 9}{x^{2} + 2 x - 3} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{4 x - 12} \cdot \frac{2 x^{2} - 3 x - 9}{x^{2} + 2 x - 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{4 x - 12} \cdot \frac{2 x^{2} - 3 x - 9}{x^{2} + 2 x - 3}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 4} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 4}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 2\right) \left(2 x + 3\right)}{4 \left(x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 247 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{5 x - 20} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 16 x + 16}{x^{2} - 6 x + 8} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{5 x - 20} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 16 x + 16}{x^{2} - 6 x + 8}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{5 x - 20} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 16 x + 16}{x^{2} - 6 x + 8}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 5} \div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 5}\div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot 5\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{5 \left(3 x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 248 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 4}{2 x - 10} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 19 x - 5}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 4}{2 x - 10} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 19 x - 5}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 4}{2 x - 10} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 19 x - 5}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 2} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 2}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 4\right) \left(4 x + 1\right)}{2 \left(x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 249 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10}{3 x - 18} \div \frac{2 x^{2} + 6 x + 4}{x^{2} - 7 x + 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10}{3 x - 18} \div \frac{2 x^{2} + 6 x + 4}{x^{2} - 7 x + 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10}{3 x - 18} \div \frac{2 x^{2} + 6 x + 4}{x^{2} - 7 x + 6}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 3} \div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 3}\div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 6\right)}\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 6\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 2 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{6 \left(x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 250 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}{4 x - 8} \cdot \frac{3 x^{2} - 3 x - 6}{x^{2} + x - 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}{4 x - 8} \cdot \frac{3 x^{2} - 3 x - 6}{x^{2} + x - 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}{4 x - 8} \cdot \frac{3 x^{2} - 3 x - 6}{x^{2} + x - 6}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 3 \left(x + 1\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{3 \left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{4 \left(x - 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 251 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 28}{5 x - 15} \div \frac{4 x^{2} + 20 x + 16}{x^{2} - 6 x + 9} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 28}{5 x - 15} \div \frac{4 x^{2} + 20 x + 16}{x^{2} - 6 x + 9}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 28}{5 x - 15} \div \frac{4 x^{2} + 20 x + 16}{x^{2} - 6 x + 9}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 5} \div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 5}\div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot 5\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 4 \left(x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 7\right)}{20 \left(x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 252 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 3 x + 2}{2 x - 8} \cdot \frac{2 x^{2} - 7 x - 4}{x^{2} - 1} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 3 x + 2}{2 x - 8} \cdot \frac{2 x^{2} - 7 x - 4}{x^{2} - 1}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 3 x + 2}{2 x - 8} \cdot \frac{2 x^{2} - 7 x - 4}{x^{2} - 1}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 2} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 2}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 2\right) \left(2 x + 1\right)}{2 \left(x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 253 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{3 x - 15} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 8 x + 4}{x^{2} - 7 x + 10} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{3 x - 15} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 8 x + 4}{x^{2} - 7 x + 10}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{3 x - 15} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 8 x + 4}{x^{2} - 7 x + 10}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 3} \div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 3}\div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{3 \left(3 x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 254 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{4 x - 24} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 21 x - 18}{x^{2} - 9} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{4 x - 24} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 21 x - 18}{x^{2} - 9}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{4 x - 24} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 21 x - 18}{x^{2} - 9}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 4} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 4}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 6\right)}\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 6\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 4\right) \left(4 x + 3\right)}{4 \left(x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 255 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{5 x - 10} \div \frac{2 x^{2} + 12 x + 16}{x^{2} - 3 x + 2} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{5 x - 10} \div \frac{2 x^{2} + 12 x + 16}{x^{2} - 3 x + 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{5 x - 10} \div \frac{2 x^{2} + 12 x + 16}{x^{2} - 3 x + 2}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 5} \div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 5}\div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 5\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{10 \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 256 - Resolver}

\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 6}{2 x - 6} \cdot \frac{3 x^{2} - 8 x - 3}{x^{2} - x - 2}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 2} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 2}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 6\right) \left(3 x + 1\right)}{2 \left(x - 2\right)}}}
