\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 257 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}{3 x - 12} \div \frac{4 x^{2} + 10 x + 4}{x^{2} - 7 x + 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}{3 x - 12} \div \frac{4 x^{2} + 10 x + 4}{x^{2} - 7 x + 12}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}{3 x - 12} \div \frac{4 x^{2} + 10 x + 4}{x^{2} - 7 x + 12}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3} \div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3}\div \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot 3\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x + 7\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot 3\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 7\right)}{6 \left(2 x + 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 258 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{4 x - 20} \cdot \frac{2 x^{2} - 7 x - 15}{x^{2} + 2 x - 3} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{4 x - 20} \cdot \frac{2 x^{2} - 7 x - 15}{x^{2} + 2 x - 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{4 x - 20} \cdot \frac{2 x^{2} - 7 x - 15}{x^{2} + 2 x - 3}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 4} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 4}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot 4\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot 4\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 2\right) \left(2 x + 3\right)}{4 \left(x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 259 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{5 x - 30} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 16 x + 16}{x^{2} - 8 x + 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{5 x - 30} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 16 x + 16}{x^{2} - 8 x + 12}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{5 x - 30} \div \frac{3 x^{2} + 16 x + 16}{x^{2} - 8 x + 12}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 5} \div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 5}\div \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 5}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot 5\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 6\right)}\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 6\right)}\cdot 5\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(3 x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{5 \left(3 x + 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 260 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 4}{2 x - 4} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 7 x - 2}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 4}{2 x - 4} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 7 x - 2}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 4}{2 x - 4} \cdot \frac{4 x^{2} - 7 x - 2}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 2} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 2}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot 2\cdot \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \left(4 x + 1\right)}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot 2\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 4\right) \left(4 x + 1\right)}{2 \left(x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\nivel{4}{avanzado}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 261 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 12 x + 8}{x^{2} + x - 6} \div \frac{3 x^{2} - 12}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 12 x + 8}{x^{2} + x - 6} \div \frac{3 x^{2} - 12}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 12 x + 8}{x^{2} + x - 6} \div \frac{3 x^{2} - 12}{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)} \div \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}\div \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(3 x - 2\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 4\right) \left(3 x - 2\right)}{3 \left(x - 2\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 262 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 36 x + 27}{x^{2} + x - 12} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{4 x^{2} - 36} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 36 x + 27}{x^{2} + x - 12} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{4 x^{2} - 36}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 36 x + 27}{x^{2} + x - 12} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{4 x^{2} - 36}= \frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(4 x - 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 5\right) \left(4 x - 3\right)}{4 \left(x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 263 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 32 x + 64}{x^{2} + x - 20} \div \frac{5 x^{2} - 80}{x^{2} + 11 x + 30} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 32 x + 64}{x^{2} + x - 20} \div \frac{5 x^{2} - 80}{x^{2} + 11 x + 30}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 32 x + 64}{x^{2} + x - 20} \div \frac{5 x^{2} - 80}{x^{2} + 11 x + 30}= \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)} \div \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}\div \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}= \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 2 \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{2 \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{5 \left(x - 4\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 264 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{3} - x^{2} - 3 x + 1}{x^{2} + x - 30} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}{6 x^{2} - 6} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} - x^{2} - 3 x + 1}{x^{2} + x - 30} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}{6 x^{2} - 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} - x^{2} - 3 x + 1}{x^{2} + x - 30} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}{6 x^{2} - 6}= \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 6}= \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 6}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 6\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 6\right)}\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 3\right) \left(3 x - 1\right)}{6 \left(x - 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 265 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 16 x + 8}{x^{2} - 4 x - 12} \div \frac{2 x^{2} - 8}{x^{2} + 6 x + 8} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 16 x + 8}{x^{2} - 4 x - 12} \div \frac{2 x^{2} - 8}{x^{2} + 6 x + 8}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 16 x + 8}{x^{2} - 4 x - 12} \div \frac{2 x^{2} - 8}{x^{2} + 6 x + 8}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)} \div \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}\div \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 4\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)}{x - 6}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 266 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 18 x + 27}{x^{2} + 2 x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}{3 x^{2} - 27} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 18 x + 27}{x^{2} + 2 x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}{3 x^{2} - 27}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 18 x + 27}{x^{2} + 2 x - 3} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}{3 x^{2} - 27}= \frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 5\right) \left(2 x - 3\right)}{3 \left(x - 1\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 267 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 48 x + 64}{x^{2} + 2 x - 8} \div \frac{4 x^{2} - 64}{x^{2} + 10 x + 24} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 48 x + 64}{x^{2} + 2 x - 8} \div \frac{4 x^{2} - 64}{x^{2} + 10 x + 24}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} - 48 x + 64}{x^{2} + 2 x - 8} \div \frac{4 x^{2} - 64}{x^{2} + 10 x + 24}= \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)} \div \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}\div \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}= \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(3 x - 4\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 6\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 4}}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 268 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{3} - x^{2} - 4 x + 1}{x^{2} + 2 x - 15} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}{5 x^{2} - 5} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]

\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} - x^{2} - 4 x + 1}{x^{2} + 2 x - 15} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}{5 x^{2} - 5}= \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 3\right) \left(4 x - 1\right)}{5 \left(x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 8 x + 8}{x^{2} + 2 x - 24} \div \frac{6 x^{2} - 24}{x^{2} + 10 x + 24}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)} \div \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 6}{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 6}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 6}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 6}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 2 \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 6\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 6\right)}\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 6}}

\vspace{0.12cm}

\vspace{0.12cm}

\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{3} - x^{2} - 3 x + 1}{x^{2} - 2 x - 15} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 9}{3 x^{2} - 3}= \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(3 x - 1\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 3\right) \left(3 x - 1\right)}{3 \left(x - 5\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 16 x + 8}{x^{2} - 2 x - 24} \div \frac{4 x^{2} - 16}{x^{2} + 8 x + 16} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]

\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 16 x + 8}{x^{2} - 2 x - 24} \div \frac{4 x^{2} - 16}{x^{2} + 8 x + 16}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)} \div \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}\div \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot 4}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 2 \left(2 x - 1\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x + 4\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot 4}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 4\right) \left(2 x - 1\right)}{2 \left(x - 6\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{3} - 3 x^{2} - 18 x + 27}{x^{2} + 4 x - 5} \cdot \frac{x^{2} + 10 x + 25}{5 x^{2} - 45}= \frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(2 x - 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(x + 5\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(x - 1\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot 5}}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot \left(4 x - 1\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x + 3\right)}{\cancel{\left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot 2}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{\frac{\left(x + 3\right) \left(4 x - 1\right)}{2 \left(x - 3\right)}}}
\end{enumerate}

\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 281 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{3 x^{4} + 11 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} - 44 x - 24}{x y + 2 x - 3 y - 6} \div \frac{x^{3} + 3 x^{2} - 4 x - 12}{3 x y + 6 x - 9 y - 18} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{4} + 11 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} - 44 x - 24}{x y + 2 x - 3 y - 6} \div \frac{x^{3} + 3 x^{2} - 4 x - 12}{3 x y + 6 x - 9 y - 18}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{4} + 11 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} - 44 x - 24}{x y + 2 x - 3 y - 6} \div \frac{x^{3} + 3 x^{2} - 4 x - 12}{3 x y + 6 x - 9 y - 18}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot 3}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot 3}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 2\right)}\cdot 3}{\cancel{\left(x - 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{3 \left(3 x + 2\right)}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 282 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{4} + 19 x^{3} - 24 x^{2} - 171 x - 108}{x y + 3 x - 4 y - 12} \cdot \frac{4 x y + 12 x - 16 y - 48}{x^{3} + 4 x^{2} - 9 x - 36} $\par

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 3\right)}\cdot 4}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}}}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 283 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{2 x^{4} + 14 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} - 224 x - 320}{x y + x - 5 y - 5} \div \frac{x^{3} + 5 x^{2} - 16 x - 80}{5 x y + 5 x - 25 y - 25} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]

\item \fitmath{\frac{2 x^{4} + 14 x^{3} - 12 x^{2} - 224 x - 320}{x y + x - 5 y - 5} \div \frac{x^{3} + 5 x^{2} - 16 x - 80}{5 x y + 5 x - 25 y - 25}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot 5}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)}\div \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot 5}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot 5}{\left(x - 5\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot 2 \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 1\right)}\cdot 5}{\cancel{\left(x - 5\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 1\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}}
\item \fitmath{\boxed{10 \left(x + 2\right)}}
\end{enumerate}

\vspace{0.12cm}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot 2}{\left(x - 6\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}

\vspace{0.12cm}

\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{4} + 14 x^{3} + 2 x^{2} - 14 x - 6}{x y + 3 x - 7 y - 21} \div \frac{x^{3} + 3 x^{2} - x - 3}{3 x y + 9 x - 21 y - 63} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4} + 14 x^{3} + 2 x^{2} - 14 x - 6}{x y + 3 x - 7 y - 21} \div \frac{x^{3} + 3 x^{2} - x - 3}{3 x y + 9 x - 21 y - 63}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4} + 14 x^{3} + 2 x^{2} - 14 x - 6}{x y + 3 x - 7 y - 21} \div \frac{x^{3} + 3 x^{2} - x - 3}{3 x y + 9 x - 21 y - 63}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 7\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 7\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot 3}}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 7\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)}\cdot \frac{\left(x - 7\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)\cdot 2 \left(2 x + 1\right)\cdot \left(x - 7\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot 3}{\left(x - 7\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 2\right)\cdot \left(y + 1\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot \left(2 x + 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 1\right)}\cdot 4}{\cancel{\left(x - 2\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 1\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}}}

\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{4} + 11 x^{3} - 42 x^{2} - 176 x - 96}{x y + 2 x - 3 y - 6} \div \frac{x^{3} + 3 x^{2} - 16 x - 48}{3 x y + 6 x - 9 y - 18}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{3 x^{4} + 11 x^{3} - 42 x^{2} - 176 x - 96}{x y + 2 x - 3 y - 6} \div \frac{x^{3} + 3 x^{2} - 16 x - 48}{3 x y + 6 x - 9 y - 18}= \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(3 x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)} \div \frac{\left(x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 3\right)\cdot \left(y + 2\right)\cdot 3}}

\item \fitmath{\boxed{3 \left(3 x + 2\right)}}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 294 - Resolver}
\textbf{Enunciado:} $ \frac{4 x^{4} + 19 x^{3} - 88 x^{2} - 475 x - 300}{x y + 3 x - 4 y - 12} \cdot \frac{4 x y + 12 x - 16 y - 48}{x^{3} + 4 x^{2} - 25 x - 100} $\par
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em]
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4} + 19 x^{3} - 88 x^{2} - 475 x - 300}{x y + 3 x - 4 y - 12} \cdot \frac{4 x y + 12 x - 16 y - 48}{x^{3} + 4 x^{2} - 25 x - 100}}
\item \fitmath{\frac{4 x^{4} + 19 x^{3} - 88 x^{2} - 475 x - 300}{x y + 3 x - 4 y - 12} \cdot \frac{4 x y + 12 x - 16 y - 48}{x^{3} + 4 x^{2} - 25 x - 100}= \frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)} \cdot \frac{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}

\item \fitmath{\frac{\left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot 4}{\left(x - 4\right)\cdot \left(y + 3\right)\cdot \left(x + 4\right)\cdot \left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)}= \frac{\cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)}\cdot \left(4 x + 3\right)\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 3\right)}\cdot 4}{\cancel{\left(x - 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(y + 3\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x + 4\right)}\cdot \cancel{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}}

\vspace{0.12cm}
\subsubsection*{Ejercicio 295 - Resolver}

\item \fitmath{\boxed{10 \left(x + 2\right)}}

\vspace{0.12cm}

\vspace{0.12cm}

\vspace{0.12cm}
